Static-analysis context for repository-level code navigation. Use the source links to verify details. Call edges are analyzer-resolved relationships, not complete runtime traces.

## Analyzer Architecture Summary

- models-as-a-service is a Kubernetes component.
- deploying 5 workload(s) (maas-api, maas-discovery, payload-processing, maas-api, maas-controller) with 5 container(s).
- watching 12 resource type(s).
- exposing 5 service(s) on ports\: 0, 443, 8080, 8443, 9004.
- Dockerfile EXPOSE ports\: 8080, 8443.
- with 5 webhook(s) (5 validating).
- protected by 14 network policy/policies.
- runtime dependencies\: OpenTelemetry Collector, PostgreSQL, kubernetes, maas-api.
- with 597 operator config constants (5 image references, 1 env vars).
- exposing 10 Prometheus metrics.
- with 2 ingress route(s).
- Python k8s client with 2 API call(s).
- integrates with management relationship via labels/annotations.
- references components\: kserve (import, provider).
- mounts 2 ConfigMap(s) as volumes\: openshift-service-ca.crt, payload-processing-plugins.
